<div class="grve-section grve-fullwidth grve-bg-none grve-feature-header grve-feature-footer grve-equal-column grve-custom-height hide-background-wrapper" style="padding-bottom: 120px;"> <div class="grve-container"> <div class="grve-row grve-bookmark"><div class="wpb_column grve-column grve-column-2-3 vc_custom_1570045629883 grve-tablet-column-3-4 grve-tablet-sm-column-1"><div class="vc_empty_space hide-on-mobile" style="height: 200px"><span class="vc_empty_space_inner"></span></div><h2 class="grve-element grve-title grve-align-left grve-h2 thin" style=""><span>Simplify your Security<span class="grve-title-line grve-bg-primary-2" style="width: 150px;height: 2px;"></span></span></h2><div class="grve-element grve-text"><p>Make secure email and file sharing easier for everyone:</p>
<ul>
<li>Single sign-on, inbox delivery, Outlook plug-ins</li>
